Bessarabian Village Death Records, 188x (R. Drefs)

        Published by the Odessa Digital Library - 17 Jun 1999
             http://www.odessa3.org

        This file contains death records from the Bessarabian villages
        of South Russia for the period 1880-1885. (No records are available
        for 1880)  This information was compiled by R. Reuben Drefs,
        coordinator and volunteer Rose Marie Reimer from the St. Petersburg
        Lutheran Evangelical archives published by the LDS.
        Edited by Marty McMahon.
